## Reading the inventory

Every row supplies the twelve requested fields: exact path; role; dependencies; current flow; permissions/lifecycle; native counterpart; one primary classification; rationale; risks/license; located tests; target module; proposed Gate. Shared codes are part of each row, not separate classifications. All target destinations refer to the existing `:AnkiDroid` module; no new Gradle module is proposed. `None/Never` means do not migrate, not delete reference-source files.

Inspection covered declarations, implementation bodies/contract outlines and symbol-reference sites for every production file, with complete or focused body reads for speech/audio entrypoints, wiring, native command/media ownership and transaction paths. This is a migration discovery, not a full line-by-line security audit. The per-file refs and tests are **lexical locations**, sometimes imports/comments/model use; they are navigation aids rather than proof of invocation or passing tests. Top-level Compose/functions can be missed by class-symbol indexing. Verified active call paths and unwired paths are distinguished in the [dependency map](FSA-01A-DEPENDENCY-MAP.md); tested assertions and missing tests are in the [verification plan](FSA-01A-VERIFICATION-PLAN.md).

**L1, inherited by every row:** no tracked source LICENSE/COPYING/NOTICE file or app-main SPDX/copyright grant was found at this revision. Ownership, redistribution permission, original authorship and dependency notices must be established **before source code/assets/tests are copied** into the target GPL-3.0-or-later tree. This is a future code-transfer prerequisite, not a claim of incompatibility or a blocker to source discovery. REUSE_AS_IS means algorithmically suitable, subject to licensing, package/resource adaptation and target validation; it is not permission to copy now. Third-party dependencies require their own review; do not transplant the source Gradle stack.

### Permissions and lifecycle codes

- **P0:** no runtime permission from the value/helper itself; callers own coroutine scope, time source and lifecycle. Use monotonic elapsed time for timeouts; source wall-clock helpers alone are insufficient.
- **P1:** RECORD_AUDIO and recognition-service visibility; main-thread SpeechRecognizer operations; cancel/destroy on pause, navigation, profile/card epoch change or permission loss. API31 on-device, API33 support/download and API34 language extras need guards; target min 24. Prefer-offline intent is advisory.
- **P2:** AudioManager/device callbacks and broadcasts; legacy Bluetooth permission through API30, BLUETOOTH_CONNECT on31+ where required; unregister on owner stop/destroy. Available devices do not prove physical route. No Bluetooth/phone-mic certification from source tests.
- **P3:** TTS initialization/voice availability, focus, queue cancellation and completion; native media lifecycle owns card playback. Microphone opens only after all native audio settles and a revalidated gap; target24 compatibility required.
- **P4:** source external READ_WRITE_DATABASE permission/ContentResolver and backend-qualified collection identity; replace with in-process native collection/profile lifetimes. Never infer collection isolation from package ID. Mutation variants add their own stores/locks, which must not migrate.
- **P6:** INTERNET, reconnect/request coroutines and remote data/ACK state; no target permission or consent for exporting card text/audio implied. Server identity is not native collection/profile identity.
- **P7:** extra WebView, media/file/link/JS handling and destroy lifecycle. Replace with target renderer and its security/AV policy.
- **P8:** source DataStore/encrypted prefs and app-global persistence; do not carry backend locks/tokens/profiles into native voice preferences. No transcript persistence by default.
- **P9:** bounded diagnostics/log/export scopes; redact credentials, tokens, card/audio/transcript content before logging. Regex credential redaction alone does not remove clinical or learning content.
- **P10:** Compose lifecycle collection, gestures/semantics and source ViewModel scopes; adapt to existing native screens. Disabled/processing/gesture cancellation must prevent duplicate start/rating callbacks.
- **P11:** application/Activity/startup permission ownership; source service is START_STICKY with microphone/mediaPlayback types, notification and two-hour wake lock. Background behavior requires a separate gate; no source manifest/service copy.

N1–N7 are exact native mappings in [native reuse matrix](FSA-01A-NATIVE-REUSE-MATRIX.md). Gates are prospective proposals in [migration sequence](FSA-01A-MIGRATION-SEQUENCE.md), not approvals or executed features.

## Shared flow and decision policies

Every row inherits its named policy below. Dependencies/reference paths abbreviated under the production package root expand to app/src/main/java/com/studyagent/client/. Test paths expand from app/src/. All references use the pinned source revision above.

| Policy | Current flow | Default rationale / risk |
|---|---|---|
| stt | RecognitionRequest -> Android backend events -> orchestrator result -> candidate/interpreter | Retain recognition contracts selectively; bind callbacks/retries to native turn and explicit offline policy. |
| audio | device callbacks -> snapshot -> route policy/coordinator -> voice gate/UI | Device availability is not selected capture/output routing; validate real hardware and lifecycle. |
| tts | speech request -> independent orchestrator/queue -> engine -> completion | Reuse native media/TTS ownership; do not import a second card-audio engine or queue. |
| voice | recognition or playback readiness -> source study state -> voice action | Use native presentation/phase and owned cancellation; source session authority cannot migrate. |
| anki | backend-qualified DTO/query -> external Anki backend -> projection | External-provider contracts are redundant inside the fork; native collection/profile owns data. |
| dataanki | ContentProvider probes/query/mapping -> external AnkiDroid backend -> callers | Source contract pins exported AnkiDroid 2.24.1 API; use fork internal APIs instead. |
| study | StudyEvent -> separate reducer/machine state -> Anki/network/voice effects | Replace authoritative study queue/state with native Reviewer; retain only bounded voice ideas. |
| render | external rendered-card DTO -> render controller/document -> extra WebView | Do not introduce a second renderer, HTML rewriting path or JS permission surface. |
| network | PC agent WebSocket protocol -> request/ACK/reconnect -> session/review state | Remove legacy Anki transport and remote review authority; any future AI transport needs a new bounded contract. |
| models | source server/session/settings DTO -> repositories -> projections | Split useful voice/advisory values from server identity and scheduler/session ownership. |
| preferences | source settings/profile DataStore -> app container -> speech/UI | Extract voice options only; discard backend selection, server profiles and durable session locks. |
| repository | source backend/transport/store flows -> source session/dashboard/UI | Do not port external backend or server-owned study orchestration. |
| diagnostics | bounded technical events/counters -> source diagnostic projection/export | Keep content-free bounded voice metrics; remove server/ledger authority and redact exports. |
| common | shared pure/platform utility -> source consumers | Prefer target utilities; check clock, platform API and redaction semantics before reuse. |
| ui | Compose state/callback -> source ViewModel/repository -> backend or session | Retain native application/Reviewer; source screens are not a drop-in second shell. |
| shell | Application/Activity/global container -> source services/repos/screens | Do not copy application shell, global service locator, startup permissions or toolchain. |
| service | source session -> sticky foreground service/notification/wake lock -> source repo | Separate background microphone/notification/privacy design; no two-hour wake-lock service copy. |
| security | source server token -> encrypted preferences -> connection | Provider-specific key custody/consent must be designed; no credentials required by discovery. |
